  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Casimir III, leader of Poland (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Poland
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Winged Hussar, Replacing Lancer (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Pancerny, Replacing Heavy Skirmisher (Requires technology: Physics)
        - Unique Building: Ducal Stable, Replacing Stable (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Building: Barbican, Replacing Armory (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Civilization Ability: Gain 1 Free Social Policy when you enter the Classical, Renaissance, Modern and Information Eras. Gain 2 additional Free Tenets when you adopt an Ideology for the first time.
      - Leader: Casimir III
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      
      ## Policies
      - Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Transnationalism: Each turn, increases the chance that a Corporate Franchise will appear in a foreign City to 15% (normally 5%). Global Franchise maximum increased by 25%, and every owned Town you have built counts as a Franchise. +2 Culture from all Corporation Offices.
      - Universal Healthcare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Healthcare: Receive a free Hospital in every City. +50 when a Citizen is born in any City, scaling with Era.
      - Their Finest Hour (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Their Finest Hour: All cities get +2 Air Unit Slots. Each Air Unit stationed in a city increases the City's Defense by 3. Can build B17 Bombers.
      - Self Determination (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Self Determination: Liberating a city (including removing another civ's Sphere of Influence on a City-State through conquest) gives 15 XP to all units, 50 Influence with all City-States, and 40 Science, scaling with Era and City Population. The liberated city gains an Arsenal and 6 units.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Economic Union (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Economic Union: Receive two additional Trade Routes. +6 Gold from trade routes with civilizations following Freedom.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 9
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 3
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10

        - MinorCivHostileBias: 5
        - MinorCivWarBias: 5
        - DenounceWillingness: 8
        - Forgiveness: 2
        - Meanness: 8
        - Neediness: 7
        - Chattiness: 9
        - DeceptiveBias: 4
        - Rationale: Adjusting persona for final 5 turns before WC vote. INCREASED WarBias from 2 to 5 - with nuclear weapons completing next turn, we are prepared to escalate if Egypt refuses peace. Increased Boldness to 7 and Meanness to 8 for nuclear deterrence credibility - Egypt must believe we will use atomic weapons on their forces. High Chattiness (9) maximizes diplomatic contact with Inca/Denmark/China to ensure alignment and vote coordination. These settings optimize for immediate military pressure + diplomatic consolidation.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: None (Policy)
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Buenos Aires
        - Public: 70
        - Private: 70
        - Rationale: Buenos Aires secure at 227 Ally with Foreign Service. Brazil spy detected but our influence insurmountable. Maintaining positive stance while focusing diplomatic resources on Zurich.
        - UpdatedTurn: 171
        
        ### Prague
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Prague secure as Ally (141). Egypt at 95 Friends cannot surpass us soon. Stable CS providing science via Shadow Networks. Maintaining positive relationship while prioritizing recoverable CS quests.
        - UpdatedTurn: 171
        
        ### Edinburgh
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 35
        - Rationale: Initial contact with Edinburgh (Player 30). Setting friendly-neutral stance (40/35). Currently Inca Ally at 109 influence, we're at 9 neutral. No immediate strategic value but establishes positive baseline for future influence opportunities. Compatible with our diplomatic posture toward distant CS.
        - UpdatedTurn: 190
        
        ### Onondaga
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 35
        - Rationale: Initial contact with Onondaga (Player 35). Setting friendly-neutral stance (40/35). Currently Inca Ally at 123 influence, we're at 6 with faith gift received. Onondaga provides Faith and is protected by Denmark/China. Establishing positive relations for potential future alliance if Inca influence wanes.
        - UpdatedTurn: 190
        
        ### Yerevan
        - Public: 60
        - Private: 55
        - Rationale: Reducing Yerevan priority from 85/80 to 60/55. Successfully recovered Yerevan as Ally (328 influence vs Brazil's 167). The CS is now secure with comfortable lead, though still majority Islam. Reducing but maintaining positive stance to focus diplomatic resources elsewhere, particularly Melbourne's critical spy mission quest.
        - UpdatedTurn: 211
        
        ### Zurich
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 45
        - Rationale: Reducing Zurich priority from 60/55 to 50/45. We lost Zurich to Brazil (108 vs our 102) and have DoF with Brazil, making direct competition awkward. Melbourne is higher priority with imminent spy quest. Also Brazil has Defensive Pact with Egypt, so their alliance with Zurich indirectly helps our rival. Reducing but maintaining positive stance for potential future recovery after securing Melbourne.
        - UpdatedTurn: 232
        
        ### Melbourne
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 75
        - Rationale: Reducing Melbourne priority from 100/100 to 80/75. The Sphere of Influence enacted last turn now provides permanent alliance protection regardless of Egyptian attacks. Melbourne is secure, freeing diplomatic resources for other priorities while maintaining positive relations.
        - UpdatedTurn: 362
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -10
        - Rationale: Brazil relations await. Peace maintained that allows focus on Science Victory. Cultural threat remains but timeline extended due to China resistance (50% influence). Avoid provoking war while racing for space, but remain privately cautious.
        - UpdatedTurn: 381
        
        ### The Inca
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Inca relations reaffirmed at maximum 100/100. Inca provides 8 delegates and has Defensive Pact with both Poland and Denmark, creating the core 10-delegate Freedom voting bloc. They are fighting Brazil alongside us and share Freedom ideology. Absolutely critical to maintain maximum relations to ensure their WC vote aligns with mine in 10 turns. Any deterioration risks China poaching their support or them voting independently.
        - UpdatedTurn: 387
        
        ### Denmark
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Denmark relations reaffirmed at maximum 100/100. Denmark provides 9 delegates (higher than Inca) and shares Freedom ideology with Defensive Pact active. Fighting Brazil alongside us with 24% war score. Essential component of my 47-delegate coalition (30 Poland + 8 Inca + 9 Denmark). Maximum priority to ensure cohesive Freedom bloc voting at World Congress. Their delegate count could push me over 45 threshold even if some votes are split.
        - UpdatedTurn: 387
        
        ### China
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 75
        - Rationale: Improving relations with China from 75/70 to 80/75. With World Congress vote in 5 turns, China (39 delegates, Order ideology) is the primary competitor. While difficult to convert entirely due to -999 Order/Freedom penalty, slightly warmer relations reduce risk of hostile actions that could disrupt the vote. China's 14170 military strength is formidable - avoiding provocation while consolidating my 46-delegate coalition takes priority. This signals diplomatic maturity to swing voters.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### Egypt
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -45
        - Rationale: Increasing hostility toward Egypt from -40/-35 to -50/-45. Egypt continues offensive against Melbourne despite 47% war weariness. Nuclear Fission completes next turn - this signals the AI that we are preparing massive retaliation. The increased hostility triggers more aggressive military posture against Egyptian forces. Once nuclear weapons are available, we will use them to break the Egyptian siege of Melbourne/Yerevan and force immediate peace before the WC vote. Egypt's refusal to accept peace demands escalation.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### Assyria
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: Maximizing relations with Assyria from 90/85 to 95/90. War with Assyria (0% score, 10% their weariness) continues to block 9 potential delegates and consumes military resources with only 5 turns to WC vote. Peace with Assyria is CRITICAL to unlock their delegates for potential support and free all military resources for Egyptian front. Maximum positive stance signals urgent desire for peace acceptance.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### The Zulus
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: Improving relations with Zulu from 80/75 to 90/85. War with Zulu (0% score, 20% our weariness, 19% theirs) serves no strategic purpose with World Congress vote in 5 turns. Peace would free military units for Egyptian front and reduce overall war weariness. Maximum priority to eliminate this drain before the critical vote. Zulu's Order ideology makes voting alliance impossible, but removing war is essential for focus.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Nuclear Fission completes in 1 turn - RAISED NUKE TO 95 and UseNuke to 85 for IMMEDIATE deployment against Egyptian forces at Melbourne/Yerevan to force peace before WC vote. Switched grand strategy to UnitedNations with vote in 5 turns - I have 46 delegates but need to ensure victory. Egypt at 47% war weariness - nuclear strikes will break their offensive. Defense remains at 95 against Egyptian pressure. Raised Diplomacy to 85 to consolidate all CS alliances post-war. Computers next for Space Procurements and Carrier unlock.
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Casimir III, leader of Poland (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Poland
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Winged Hussar, Replacing Lancer (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Pancerny, Replacing Heavy Skirmisher (Requires technology: Physics)
        - Unique Building: Ducal Stable, Replacing Stable (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Building: Barbican, Replacing Armory (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Civilization Ability: Gain 1 Free Social Policy when you enter the Classical, Renaissance, Modern and Information Eras. Gain 2 additional Free Tenets when you adopt an Ideology for the first time.
      - Leader: Casimir III
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      
      ## Policies
      - Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Transnationalism: Each turn, increases the chance that a Corporate Franchise will appear in a foreign City to 15% (normally 5%). Global Franchise maximum increased by 25%, and every owned Town you have built counts as a Franchise. +2 Culture from all Corporation Offices.
      - Universal Healthcare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Healthcare: Receive a free Hospital in every City. +50 when a Citizen is born in any City, scaling with Era.
      - Their Finest Hour (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Their Finest Hour: All cities get +2 Air Unit Slots. Each Air Unit stationed in a city increases the City's Defense by 3. Can build B17 Bombers.
      - Self Determination (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Self Determination: Liberating a city (including removing another civ's Sphere of Influence on a City-State through conquest) gives 15 XP to all units, 50 Influence with all City-States, and 40 Science, scaling with Era and City Population. The liberated city gains an Arsenal and 6 units.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Economic Union (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Economic Union: Receive two additional Trade Routes. +6 Gold from trade routes with civilizations following Freedom.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: Set next tech to Satellites to reveal the map and unlock the Apollo Program, keeping our Science Victory backup plan strong while we pursue a Diplomatic Victory.
        - Technology: Satellites
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: Economic Union
        - Rationale: Selecting Economic Union to gain +2 trade routes and extra gold from trade with Freedom civilizations (Inca and China), giving us the financial power to buy city-state alliances before the World Congress vote.
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Reaffirming maximum friendly relations with the Inca (our Defensive Pact ally and top UN partner) to guarantee their 8 delegates vote for us as World Leader in 4 turns.
        - Private: 100
        - TargetID: 3
        - Public: 100
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Reaffirming maximum friendly relations with Denmark (our Defensive Pact ally and top friend) to secure their 9 delegates for the upcoming World Leader vote.
        - Private: 100
        - TargetID: 5
        - Public: 100
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Increasing hostility slightly to guide the tactical AI into an aggressive naval assault to liberate the coastal city-state of Yerevan from Egyptian occupation, securing its delegates and completing Zurich's quest.
        - Private: -55
        - TargetID: 1
        - Public: -60
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Maintaining friendly relations with China to prevent any military conflict with their massive 14k army, while we actively compete with them for city-state influence.
        - Public: 80
        - TargetID: 7
        - Private: 75
    - #6:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Maintaining maximum positive stance to encourage Assyria to accept a peace treaty, which would stop the military drain and remove their negative voting bias against us.
        - Private: 90
        - TargetID: 6
        - Public: 95
    - #7:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Maintaining maximum positive stance to encourage the Zulus to accept peace, freeing up military resources and reducing war weariness.
        - TargetID: 0
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85

We raise Offense (35) to encourage our navy to immediately bombard and liberate Yerevan from Egypt. Production (95) is maxed to finish critical builds, while Nuke (95) and UseNuke (85) remain high as a deterrent now that Nuclear Fission is complete.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 60807
    - reasoningTokens: 10486
    - outputTokens: 701
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Focusing on Congress**
            
            My current focus is on securing a Diplomatic Victory, with the World Congress vote looming in just four turns. I'm strategizing to maximize our vote count, ensuring we either win outright or maintain a strong position. If necessary, I'm also preparing a contingency plan to pivot towards a Science Victory.
            
            **Analyzing Delegate Votes**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our delegate support for the upcoming World Congress vote. It appears I've misunderstood some ideological alignments; Denmark, for instance, is Autocracy, not Freedom as I initially thought. This requires a quick adjustment to my diplomatic outreach, especially considering our key allies.
            
            **Securing Delegate Support**
            
            I'm re-examining delegate counts and ideological alignments. It's crucial to understand that while China shares my ideology and is a key competitor for the UN, their significant delegate count demands careful consideration. I'm now prioritizing alliances with critical city-states to bolster our vote.
            
            **Confirming Diplomatic Allies**
            
            I'm reconfirming the status of our city-state allies. Melbourne, Prague, and Venice are secured through our Sphere of Influence. Buenos Aires is contested, and I'm addressing this immediately. Eraclea and Antwerp are unfortunately aligned with China.
            
            **Revising Diplomatic Strategy**
            
            I'm recalculating delegate strengths, noting China's strong hold on Milan and Edinburgh due to their spheres. A key proposal could free Milan, so I'm focusing on supporting Poland's repeal initiative to potentially shift those delegates in our favor, while also considering the impact on Zurich's openness.
            
            **Analyzing Delegate Disparity**
            
            China currently holds a significant delegate lead. I'm assessing how to overcome this, considering options like securing additional city-state alliances or influencing Denmark and the Inca. The World Leader vote mechanics require careful re-evaluation to secure the necessary 45 delegates.
            
            **Setting Next Research**
            
            I've decided to prioritize "Satellites" as our next research target following "Computers." This choice strengthens my Science Victory contingency and provides crucial map information, though "Telecommunications" for its tourism benefits was a close contender.
            
            **Reviewing Policy Choices**
            
            I'm currently reviewing our policy options within the Freedom ideology. Having adopted ten tenets already, I'm evaluating the remaining Tier 1 and Tier 2 choices, considering "Civil Society," "Economic Union," "Universal Healthcare," "Their Finest Hour," "Transnationalism," and "Self Determination" to best advance our position.
            
            **Refining Policy Choices**
            
            I'm prioritizing "Economic Union" for its trade route benefits, especially trading with Freedom civs like China and Inca. "Civil Society" is also appealing for its growth and specialist bonuses. "Free Thought" and "Empiricism" from Rationalism seem essential for accelerating our science output.
            
            **Choosing Next Policy**
            
            I've decided on "Economic Union" as my next policy choice. Its significant boosts to trade routes and gold generation are crucial, especially for expanding our influence and supporting a diplomatic victory. I'm also re-evaluating our ongoing war with Egypt, as their naval actions have been costly.
            
            **Liberating Yerevan**
            
            I must liberate Yerevan from Egypt's control to fulfill Zurich's quest and secure their alliance. Egypt's aggressive expansion, evidenced by their occupation of Yerevan and attack on Melbourne, necessitates a strong response. This situation also highlights a weakness in our defensive posture.
            
            **Liberating Yerevan**
            
            I'm focusing on liberating Yerevan. Although heavily defended by Egypt with artillery, air units, and landships, our nearby forces at Buenos Aires, bolstered by Polish support, are substantial. Securing Yerevan will grant us significant influence with Zurich and bring them into our alliance.
            
            **Developing Atomic Capabilities**
            
            I am now evaluating the feasibility of developing and deploying atomic bombs. With four Uranium available, I am assessing production options in Warsaw or Wroclaw to target Yerevan or Egyptian forces directly. I need to verify Warsaw's current production queue and its ability to prioritize an Atomic Bomb.
            
            **Securing Critical Allies**
            
            My focus has shifted to a critical threat: Buenos Aires is dangerously close to falling under China's influence. I'm dispatching an Ambassador from Wroclaw immediately to secure this vital city-state. Simultaneously, I'm deploying agents to Egypt to gather intelligence and potentially complete quests that will bolster our influence.
            
            **Completing Spy Missions**
            
            I'm prioritizing completing spy missions for Prague, Zurich, Stockholm, Eraclea, and Buenos Aires, as these offer substantial influence boosts and potential alliances. The ongoing wars with Assyria and Zulu are also being re-evaluated due to their negative diplomatic standing.
            
            **Assessing Peace Opportunities**
            
            I am re-evaluating the current wars, particularly with Egypt. Given our military superiority and their high war weariness, I believe there's an opportunity to negotiate a favorable peace. This could free up resources and potentially gain us their future support or at least neutralize a threat before the World Congress.
            
            **Stopping Brazil's Victory**
            
            Brazil is alarmingly close to a Cultural Victory, being influential with numerous civs and nearing influential status with Egypt and Poland. I'm assessing how to counter this threat, noting our expiring peace treaty with them as a potential leverage point for war with allies.
            
            **Stopping Brazil's Victory**
            
            I'm analyzing how to thwart Brazil's Cultural Victory. Given Denmark is already at war with them and we share a different ideology, I'm focusing on how our high culture output and existing sanctions can counter their influence. Military action is also an option.
            
            **Monitoring Brazil's Victory**
            
            I'm closely watching Brazil's Cultural Victory progress. With our peace treaty expiring soon, I'm preparing for potential conflict to contain their influence, while maintaining cautious relations for now. China remains a significant diplomatic rival with a substantial delegate lead that needs addressing.
            
            **Calculating Delegate Support**
            
            I'm recalculating delegate allocations for the UN vote. China appears poised to garner significant support from warring or hostile nations. I'm prioritizing solidifying Inca and Denmark's votes, despite ideological differences, and assessing if Egypt, Brazil, Assyria, and Zulu's votes can be swayed or negated to prevent China's win.
            
            **Securing Delegates**
            
            I'm focusing on undermining China's delegate advantage. Their strong alliances with Genoa, Eraclea, and Edinburgh are challenging, but Antwerp and Milan present opportunities. I need to secure enough influence to flip Antwerp and prepare for Milan's potential repeal to shift delegates our way.
            
            **Securing Critical City-States**
            
            I'm prioritizing Buenos Aires; China's influence is perilously close to ours, and losing it means losing valuable delegates. I'm also assessing Antwerp and Zanzibar for potential flips, as we need to counter China's delegate advantage. Sending our Ambassador and using gold are key immediate actions.
            
            **Completing Quests**
            
            I'm now prioritizing quests that boost city-state influence, particularly Zurich's quest to liberate Yerevan. This will grant significant influence, bringing them into our alliance and potentially impacting the upcoming World Congress vote. I'm also assessing our gold reserves for potential gifts.
            
            **Liberating Yerevan with Navy**
            
            My naval forces are positioned to bombard and liberate Yerevan. With Dreadnoughts and Destroyers, I can attack the coastal city directly, overcoming Egypt's land defenses. This action secures Zurich's alliance and reclaims Yerevan, significantly impacting the World Congress.
            
            **Bombarding Yerevan's Defenses**
            
            My naval fleet is perfectly situated to bombard Yerevan. With multiple Dreadnoughts and Destroyers adjacent to the city, I can reduce its defenses rapidly. This maneuver will liberate the city, secure Zurich's crucial alliance, and grant significant delegate influence for the World Congress.
            
            **Adopting "Self Determination"**
            
            I've realized a powerful synergy: adopting "Self Determination" immediately before liberating Yerevan will grant an immense 50 influence with all city-states. This will dramatically bolster our position with Buenos Aires, Antwerp, Zanzibar, and Milan, significantly impacting the World Congress delegate count.
            
            **Revising Policy Timelines**
            
            My next policy adoption, "Self Determination," is now slated for five turns from now, which is one turn *after* the crucial World Congress vote. This means I cannot leverage its city-state influence boost for this immediate vote. I must re-evaluate my priorities, considering that Yerevan's liberation, while important, will occur without this policy’s immediate benefit.
            
            **Choosing Next Policy**
            
            My immediate focus is on leveraging "Economic Union" for its trade and gold benefits, as it's a Tier 1 tenet I can adopt now. This decision acknowledges the urgency of the upcoming World Congress vote, requiring me to forgo the city-state influence boost from "Self Determination" for a later date to secure needed delegates.
            
            **Leveraging Gold and Alliances**
            
            I'm realizing the immediate power of gold. I can now gift it to city-states to buy influence directly, especially since "Economic Union" will boost our gold reserves significantly. This allows me to secure delegates before the World Congress, bypassing the timing issue with "Self Determination." I'm confirming our strong relationships with Denmark and Inca.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Stances**
            
            I'm reassessing my diplomatic relationships, particularly with Egypt, Assyria, and the Zulu. While I initially considered peace to garner votes, maintaining hostility with Egypt for Yerevan's liberation is strategic. I'm also focusing on securing Buenos Aires, as its contested status requires immediate attention, and confirming our Ambassador's deployment.
            
            **Leveraging Persona to Secure Buenos Aires**
            
            I've realized that Buenos Aires can't be directly affected by the "non-city-state civilization" action. However, its persona, particularly its high MinorCivCompetitiveness and WorkAgainstWillingness, will make the AI aggressively compete for city-states. I'll exploit this to my advantage by focusing on gold and diplomatic pressure.
            
            **Developing Atomic Strategy**
            
            My current focus is on the potential use of atomic bombs. With four Uranium resources, I'm evaluating the tactical advantage of nuking Egypt's forces near Yerevan to secure the city and Zurich's alliance. I need to consider the proximity of our own naval units to the target to mitigate potential self-damage.
            
            **Prioritizing Global Influence**
            
            I'm reassessing my strategic priorities to focus on immediate gains. I've significantly boosted my inclinations towards diplomacy and gold generation to secure city-state alliances and have sufficient funds for direct influence. Offense is also being elevated to ensure Yerevan's liberation, while defense remains paramount for border security. Production is also a high priority for quick build completion.
            
            **Unlocking Atomic Power**
            
            My research into Nuclear Fission is complete, granting me atomic capabilities. My current priority is securing the World Leader vote in four turns. I'm strategizing to liberate Yerevan with my positioned navy, secure Buenos Aires with an ambassador, leverage gold for city-state influence, and maintain strong ties with Denmark and Inca to reach the 45-delegate threshold.
            
            **Researching Satellites and Gold**
            
            I'm setting research to "Satellites" to keep my Science victory backup on track. My next policy will be "Economic Union" to boost gold and trade routes, which I'll use to solidify alliances with the Inca and Denmark for crucial UN votes. I'm also adjusting diplomatic stances with Egypt.

        - #2:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: 9WrqZCqtYEMJ3EJJ
          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Policy: Economic Union
            - Rationale: Selecting Economic Union to gain +2 trade routes and extra gold from trade with Freedom civilizations (Inca and China), giving us the financial power to buy city-state alliances before the World Congress vote.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: d8KH05bpE4VAP3cC
          - toolName: set-relationship

            - Public: 100
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined

        - #5:

          - toolCallId: jTapcCgbIw3n6QoX
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - Rationale: Increasing hostility slightly to guide the tactical AI into an aggressive naval assault to liberate the coastal city-state of Yerevan from Egyptian occupation, securing its delegates and completing Zurich's quest.
            - Private: -55
            - TargetID: 1
            - Public: -60

          - providerOptions: undefined

          - input:
            - Rationale: Maintaining friendly relations with China to prevent any military conflict with their massive 14k army, while we actively compete with them for city-state influence.

            - Private: 75
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined

        - #9:

          - toolCallId: FsJC6cA0F5w5AfEE
          - toolName: set-flavors
          - input:
            - Rationale: With the World Leader vote in 4 turns, we maximize Diplomacy (95) and Gold (85) to secure city-state alliances, particularly Buenos Aires (where we lead by only 2 influence). We raise Offense (35) to encourage our navy to immediately bombard and liberate Yerevan from Egypt. Production (95) is maxed to finish critical builds, while Nuke (95) and UseNuke (85) remain high as a deterrent now that Nuclear Fission is complete.
